# A-2 CLI hashing and YAML observations

One instrumented measured process per route, not cProfile. Only the CLI process is instrumented; its launcher subprocess, already-running Runtime/host, native direct/OpenSSL constructors and unobserved file-read APIs are blind spots. SHA-256 and file_digest are separate: file_digest time includes file reads and must not be summed as pure hash time. Path.read_bytes correlations use retained object identity, never proximity; file_digest uses its stream name. Unmatched paths remain unknown. Fixture basename paths may alias and are excluded from distinct/repeat claims. Metadata equality means device/inode/size/mtime/ctime matched at observations, not that contents are equal. Observer hooks, up to 8 MiB retained data, YAML preload and JSON logging inside process-exit wall add overhead. Observer self time below covers only part of SHA wrapper bookkeeping, not the whole observer. Durations are sums of observed calls, may overlap across threads and may double-count work inherited by copied hash objects; they do not partition wall or establish savings. Byte totals omit unknown byte counts.

## W
